# Agents · A · part 11

1,015 entries, architect to architecture-advisor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **architect**: Deep design reasoning for choices with more than one defensible answer — schema shape, module boundary, protocol, adopt-vs-build, public surface. Use before a design lock is hard to reverse. Returns a structured decision record with options, tradeoffs, and a recommendation; never writes files. ([millhq/mill](https://github.com/millhq/mill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/architect.md) · ★1)
